  (PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.runtimeHaltKind SOnly38.controller.haltKind
        (PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.run SOnly38.controller.machine (SOnly38.controller.stoppingTime term)
            (SOnly38.controller.initial term)).control).isSome =
    true
'SOnly38.every_input_terminal' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnly38.selected_one_contraction (source target : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (selected : SOnly38.selector source = some target) :
  have final := SOnly38.controller.invokeRun () source;
  PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.runtimeHaltKind SOnly38.controller.haltKind final.control =
      some PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.HaltKind.redex ∧
    final.cursor.erase = target ∧
      PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.runMutationCount SOnly38.controller.machine
            (SOnly38.controller.stoppingTime source) (SOnly38.controller.initial source) =
          1 ∧
        source.contractAt? (PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.cursorAddress final.cursor) = some target
'SOnly38.selected_one_contraction' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnly38.rejected_zero_contractions (source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (selected : SOnly38.selector source = none) :
  have final := SOnly38.controller.invokeRun () source;
  PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.runtimeHaltKind SOnly38.controller.haltKind final.control =
      some PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.HaltKind.nf ∧
    PureSFormal.Research.RootResetSelectorContract.AddressNormal source ∧
      final.cursor.erase = source ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.runMutationCount SOnly38.controller.machine
            (SOnly38.controller.stoppingTime source) (SOnly38.controller.initial source) =
          0
'SOnly38.rejected_zero_contractions' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

  (decoded :

      some (horizon, config)) :

SOnlyProvenance.Descendant : PureSFormal.PureS.Term → PureSFormal.PureS.Term → Prop
'SOnlyProvenance.Descendant' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Descendant.app_cases {l r t : PureSFormal.PureS.Term} (h : SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (l.app r) t) :
  t = l.app r ∨ SOnlyProvenance.Descendant l t ∨ SOnlyProvenance.Descendant r t
'SOnlyProvenance.Descendant.app_cases' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Descendant.s_eq {t : PureSFormal.PureS.Term}
  (h : SOnlyProvenance.Descendant PureSFormal.PureS.Term.s t) : t = PureSFormal.PureS.Term.s
'SOnlyProvenance.Descendant.s_eq' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Template : Type
'SOnlyProvenance.Template' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Template.instantiate (substitution : Nat → PureSFormal.PureS.Term) :
  SOnlyProvenance.Template → PureSFormal.PureS.Term
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.instantiate' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Template.abstract (states : Nat → SOnlyProvenance.State) :
  SOnlyProvenance.Template → SOnlyProvenance.State
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.abstract' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyProvenance.Template.abstraction_exact (substitution : Nat → PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (template : SOnlyProvenance.Template) :
  SOnlyProvenance.classify (SOnlyProvenance.Template.instantiate substitution template) =
    SOnlyProvenance.Template.abstract (fun i => SOnlyProvenance.classify (substitution i)) template
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.abstraction_exact' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyProvenance.Template.holes : SOnlyProvenance.Template → List Nat
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.holes' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Template.scaffold (substitution : Nat → PureSFormal.PureS.Term) :
  SOnlyProvenance.Template → List PureSFormal.PureS.Term
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.scaffold' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyProvenance.Template.descendant_decomposition (substitution : Nat → PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (template : SOnlyProvenance.Template) {found : PureSFormal.PureS.Term}
  (occurs : SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (SOnlyProvenance.Template.instantiate substitution template) found) :
  found ∈ SOnlyProvenance.Template.scaffold substitution template ∨
    ∃ i, i ∈ template.holes ∧ SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (substitution i) found
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.descendant_decomposition' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyProvenance.Template.h6_decomposition (substitution : Nat → PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (template : SOnlyProvenance.Template) {found : PureSFormal.PureS.Term}
  (occurs : SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (SOnlyProvenance.Template.instantiate substitution template) found)
  (h6 : SOnlyProvenance.classify found = SOnlyProvenance.State.h4) :
  found ∈ SOnlyProvenance.Template.scaffold substitution template ∧
      SOnlyProvenance.classify found = SOnlyProvenance.State.h4 ∨
    ∃ i,
      i ∈ template.holes ∧
        SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (substitution i) found ∧ SOnlyProvenance.classify found = SOnlyProvenance.State.h4
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.h6_decomposition' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyProvenance.Template.transfer (substitution : Nat → PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (template : SOnlyProvenance.Template)
  (P : PureSFormal.PureS.Term → Prop)
  (scaffoldOrigins :
    ∀ (t : PureSFormal.PureS.Term),
      t ∈ SOnlyProvenance.Template.scaffold substitution template →
        SOnlyProvenance.classify t = SOnlyProvenance.State.h4 → P t)
  (holeOrigins :
    ∀ (i : Nat),
      i ∈ template.holes →
        ∀ (t : PureSFormal.PureS.Term),
          SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (substitution i) t → SOnlyProvenance.classify t = SOnlyProvenance.State.h4 → P t)
  {found : PureSFormal.PureS.Term}
  (occurs : SOnlyProvenance.Descendant (SOnlyProvenance.Template.instantiate substitution template) found)
  (h6 : SOnlyProvenance.classify found = SOnlyProvenance.State.h4) : P found
'SOnlyProvenance.Template.transfer' depends on axioms: [propext]

SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern_iff (source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) :
  SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool source = true ↔
    ∃ view,
      view.status = PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointDecoder.HaltStatus.fresh ∧
        view.route = SOnly38.targetRoute ∧
          view.label = SOnly38.targetLabel ∧
            PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointDecoder.LocalShape SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree view source

SOnlyEventTransfer.exactChain_sample (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (bits : List Bool)
  (initialGood :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.SampledGood program dispatcher bits 0
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerBound.bound program dispatcher)
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration program dispatcher bits)) :
  have system :=
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.SampledGood.productiveSystem program dispatcher bits
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerBound.bound program dispatcher)
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration program dispatcher bits) initialGood;
  ∀
    {terminal before :
      PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.Configuration (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Control program dispatcher)}
    {configurations :
      List
        (PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.Configuration
          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Control program dispatcher))}
    {sampleIndex index : Nat}
    {sample :
      PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.Configuration (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Control program dispatcher)},
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher) terminal before configurations →
      system.contractionRun sampleIndex = before →
        configurations[index]? = some sample → system.contractionRun (sampleIndex + index + 1) = sample
'SOnlyEventTransfer.exactChain_sample' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem (bits : List Bool) :
  PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.ProductiveSystem
    (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
'SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyEventTransfer.trajectory_eq_contractionRun (bits : List Bool) (index : Nat) :
  SOnly38.trajectory bits index = ((SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem bits).contractionRun index).cursor.erase
'SOnlyEventTransfer.trajectory_eq_contractionRun' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyEventTransfer.exactChain_rootReset_sample (bits : List Bool)
  {terminal before : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher}
  {configurations : List (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)}
  {sampleIndex index : Nat}
  {sample : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher}
  (chain :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher) terminal before configurations)
  (beforeEq : (SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem bits).contractionRun sampleIndex = before)
  (atIndex : configurations[index]? = some sample) :
  SOnly38.trajectory bits (sampleIndex + index + 1) = sample.cursor.erase

  PureSFormal.CTS.iteratePhase P n (PureSFormal.CTS.nextPhase P q) =
    PureSFormal.CTS.nextPhase P (PureSFormal.CTS.iteratePhase P n q)
'SOnlySource.iteratePhase_next' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlySource.consume_prefix (P : PureSFormal.CTS.Program) (front suffix : List Bool) (q : PureSFormal.CTS.Phase P) :
  PureSFormal.CTS.iterate P front.length { phase := q, data := front ++ suffix } =
    { phase := PureSFormal.CTS.iteratePhase P front.length q, data := suffix ++ SOnlySource.scan P q front }
'SOnlySource.consume_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlySource.consume_partial (P : PureSFormal.CTS.Program) (front suffix : List Bool) (q : PureSFormal.CTS.Phase P)
  (n : Nat) (hn : n ≤ front.length) :
  PureSFormal.CTS.iterate P n { phase := q, data := front ++ suffix } =
    { phase := PureSFormal.CTS.iteratePhase P n q,
      data := List.drop n front ++ suffix ++ SOnlySource.scan P q (List.take n front) }
'SOnlySource.consume_partial' depends on axioms: [propext]

  (q : PureSFormal.CTS.Phase P) (n : Nat) (hn : n < front.length) :
  (PureSFormal.CTS.ordinaryStep P (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate P n { phase := q, data := front ++ suffix })).isSome = true

  SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program time (SOnlySource.encode config)) ↔
    SOnlySource.Selected (SOnlySource.iterate (time / 19) config) ∧ time % 19 = 17

SOnlySource.first_event_exact (config : SOnlySource.Config) (n : Nat)
  (selected : SOnlySource.Selected (SOnlySource.iterate n config))
  (earlier : ∀ (j : Nat), j < n → ¬SOnlySource.Selected (SOnlySource.iterate j config)) :
  SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program (19 * n + 17) (SOnlySource.encode config)) ∧
    ∀ (time : Nat),
      time < 19 * n + 17 → ¬SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program time (SOnlySource.encode config))

  SOnlyCounter.copies word (n + 1) = word ++ SOnlyCounter.copies word n

  SOnlyCounter.copies word (m + n) = SOnlyCounter.copies word m ++ SOnlyCounter.copies word n

  SOnlyCounter.pass take (SOnlyCounter.copies [a, b] n) =
    SOnlyCounter.copies (if take = true then SOnlySource.production a else SOnlySource.production b) n

SOnlyEventResponse.response_final_sample (inputBits : List Bool)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers SOnly38.program) (bit : Bool) (seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation carrier : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame)
  (sampleIndex bridgeTicks : Nat)
  (before : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
  (beforeEq : (SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem inputBits).contractionRun sampleIndex = before)
  (bridge :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.ZeroMutationRun
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher) bridgeTicks before
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.responseStartConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher registers bit
        seedBits continuation carrier parents)) :
  SOnly38.trajectory inputBits
      (sampleIndex +
        PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completedCost SOnly38.program (SOnly38.dispatcher.route (registers.phase, bit))
          (registers.phase, bit)) =
    PureSFormal.PureS.Cursor.rebuild parents
      (PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completed seedBits continuation carrier
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.completedRoute SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher registers bit carrier))

'SOnlyObserver.event' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyObserver.event_iff_matches (term : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) :
  SOnlyObserver.event term = true ↔
    ∃ address subtree, term.subterm? address = some subtree ∧ SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool subtree = true
'SOnlyObserver.event_iff_matches' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyObserver.event_iff (term : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) :
  SOnlyObserver.event term = true ↔
    ∃ address subtree,
      term.subterm? address = some subtree ∧
        ∃ view,
          view.status = PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointDecoder.HaltStatus.fresh ∧
            view.route = SOnly38.targetRoute ∧
              view.label = SOnly38.targetLabel ∧
                PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointDecoder.LocalShape SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree view subtree
'SOnlyObserver.event_iff' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyObserver.event_finite_cover (term : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) :
  SOnlyObserver.run SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern term ∈ SOnlyObserver.stateCover SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern ∧
    (SOnlyObserver.stateCover SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern).length = 2 ^ (SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.size + 1)
'SOnlyObserver.event_finite_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyStageEvents.selectedResponse_chain (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (ticks : Nat)
  (trace :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source admissible
      registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents ticks)
  (notSeen : registers.seen = false) :
  ∃ configurations last,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.selectedReturnConfiguration program dispatcher registers bit suffix seedBits
          continuation (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext) parents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
            (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
              parents)))
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length =
          1 +
            PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completedCost program
              (dispatcher.route ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit))
              ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit) ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerTraceAlgebra.LastSample configurations last ∧
          last.cursor.erase =
            PureSFormal.PureS.Cursor.rebuild parents
              (PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completed seedBits continuation
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext)
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.completedRoute program dispatcher
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix) bit
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext)))
'SOnlyStageEvents.selectedResponse_chain' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyStageEvents.stageStartTime (bits : List Bool) : Nat → Nat
'SOnlyStageEvents.stageStartTime' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyStageEvents.stageStart_prefix (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat) :
  ∃ parents configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerRecurrence.stageSourceConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits (r + 1)
          parents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits)
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length = SOnlyStageEvents.stageStartTime bits r ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher parents
          (PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointRun.cumulativeLayers r)

SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat) : Nat
'SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyStageEvents.firstJob_response_prefix (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r →
        (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠
          []) :
  have bits := bit :: suffix;
  have environment :=
    PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree) bits;
  let continuation := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit (r + 1) r environment;
  ∃ parents registers phase responseBit responseSuffix source outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext ticks
    configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher registers
          PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
            (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits
                  continuation) ::
              parents)))
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits)
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length = SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent registers phase [] false ∧
          PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits continuation
              source ⋯ registers responseBit responseSuffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents
              ticks ∧
            { phase := phase, data := responseBit :: responseSuffix } =
              PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)
'SOnlyStageEvents.firstJob_response_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyStageEvents.sourceTarget_witness_at (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat) (eventSuffix : List Bool)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])
  (event :
    PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) =

  ∃ address shell,

      SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true ∧
        SOnlyEventTransfer.readPrestate SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree true shell = some (true :: eventSuffix)

SOnlyStageEvents.sourceEvent_reaches_observer (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (event :
    SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits))) :
  ∃ n address shell,
    SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory bits n) = true ∧
      (SOnly38.trajectory bits n).subterm? address = some shell ∧
        SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true ∧
          SOnlyEventTransfer.readPrestate SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree true shell =
            some (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data
'SOnlyStageEvents.sourceEvent_reaches_observer' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

SOnlyWitnessResult.numerical_witness (input tail : List SOnlySource.Symbol) (sourceTime value : Nat)
  (front rest : List Nat)
  (sourceAt :
    SOnlySource.iterate sourceTime { word := input, take := true } = { word := ⟨17, ⋯⟩ :: tail, take := true })
  (wordEq : 18 :: SOnlyResultBits.published tail = front ++ List.replicate (4 ^ (2 * value + 4)) 16 ++ rest)
  (no16 : ∀ (symbol : Nat), symbol ∈ front → symbol ≠ 16) (notHead : rest.head? ≠ some 16) :
  ∃ sampleIndex address shell,
    SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory (SOnlySource.encodeWord input) sampleIndex) = true ∧
      (SOnly38.trajectory (SOnlySource.encodeWord input) sampleIndex).subterm? address = some shell ∧
        SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true ∧ SOnlyWitnessResult.readShell shell = some value

SOnlySchedulerOrigins.fuel_rows_licensed {P : PureSFormal.PureS.Term → Prop} (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program) (fuel : Nat)
  {environment continuation : PureSFormal.PureS.Term} {parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame}
  (he : SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Ordinary P environment) (hb : SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Ordinary P continuation)
  (outer : SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Wraps P parents) :
  SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelPositiveFirstMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers fuel
            environment continuation parents).cursor.erase ∧
    SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelPositiveSecondMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers fuel
              environment continuation parents).cursor.erase ∧
      SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelZeroFirstMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                environment continuation parents).cursor.erase ∧
        SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
            (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelZeroSecondMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                  environment continuation parents).cursor.erase ∧
          SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
              (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelZeroThirdMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                    environment continuation parents).cursor.erase ∧
            SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelZeroFourthMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                      environment continuation parents).cursor.erase ∧
              SOnlyProvenance.AllH6 P
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.fuelZeroFifthMutationConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                      environment continuation parents).cursor.erase
'SOnlySchedulerOrigins.fuel_rows_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext]

      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher) terminal before configurations)

    PureSFormal.PureS.FiniteController.Configuration (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Control program dispatcher)}

SOnlyResponseLabels.selectedResponse_labelledChain (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (ticks : Nat)
  (trace :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source admissible
      registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents ticks)
  (notSeen : registers.seen = false) :
  ∃ configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.selectedReturnConfiguration program dispatcher registers bit suffix seedBits
          continuation (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext) parents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
            (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
              parents)))
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length =
          1 +
            PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completedCost program
              (dispatcher.route ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit))
              ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit) ∧
        SOnlyResponseLabels.OnlyLabel program dispatcher
          ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit) configurations
'SOnlyResponseLabels.selectedResponse_labelledChain' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyResponseLabels.selectedResponse_onlyLabel (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (ticks : Nat)
  (trace :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source admissible
      registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents ticks)
  (notSeen : registers.seen = false) {terminal : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher}
  {configurations : List (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher)}
  (chain :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher) terminal
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers PureSFormal.PureS.omega
        (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
              (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
            parents)))
      configurations)
  (count :
    configurations.length =
      1 +
        PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completedCost program
          (dispatcher.route ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit))
          ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit)) :
  SOnlyResponseLabels.OnlyLabel program dispatcher
    ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit) configurations

'SOnlyResponseLabels.sourceLabels_prepend' depends on axioms: [propext]
SOnlyResponseLabels.selectedNonemptyPendingPrefix_labelled (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (inputBits seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (hadmissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (outerParents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (layers : Nat)
  (outer : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher outerParents layers)
  (remaining sampleIndex : Nat) (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program)
  (phase : PureSFormal.CTS.Phase program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context) (ticks : Nat) :
  PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent registers phase [] false →
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source hadmissible
        registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext
        (PureSFormal.PureS.PrimitiveFuel.pendingParents
          (PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree) seedBits)
          continuation remaining outerParents)
        ticks →
      (∀ (k : Nat),
          k ≤ remaining → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program k { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix }).data ≠ []) →
        ∃ finalRegisters finalPhase finalBit finalSuffix finalSource finalOuterContext finalFullContext
          finalInnerContext finalTargetContext finalTicks configurations,
          PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
              (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
              (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher finalRegisters
                PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames finalFullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
                    outerParents)))
              (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
                    PureSFormal.PureS.PrimitiveFuel.pendingParents
                      (PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree)
                        seedBits)
                      continuation remaining outerParents)))
              configurations ∧
            PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.IndexedResponseSampledStates program dispatcher inputBits sampleIndex
                configurations ∧
              configurations.length =
                  PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedResponse.nonemptySweepCost program dispatcher remaining
                    { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } ∧
                PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent finalRegisters finalPhase [] false ∧
                  PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation
                      finalSource hadmissible finalRegisters finalBit finalSuffix finalOuterContext finalFullContext
                      finalInnerContext finalTargetContext outerParents finalTicks ∧
                    { phase := finalPhase, data := finalBit :: finalSuffix } =
                        PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program remaining { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } ∧
                      SOnlyResponseLabels.SourceLabels program dispatcher { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix }
                        remaining configurations
'SOnlyResponseLabels.selectedNonemptyPendingPrefix_labelled' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

SOnlyResponseLabels.completeNonemptyJob_sourceLabels (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (remaining : Nat)
  (continuation : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (layers : Nat)
  (outer : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher parents layers)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ remaining + 1 →
        (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠ [])

  {configurations : List (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher)}

    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher) terminal
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedPhase.fuelTerminalConfigurationAt program dispatcher (bit :: suffix)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers.newJob program) continuation parents (remaining + 1) 0)
      configurations)
  (count :
    configurations.length =
      PureSFormal.PureS.ExactCheckpointRun.jobCost program dispatcher (bit :: suffix) (remaining + 1)) :
  SOnlyResponseLabels.SourceLabels program dispatcher (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix)) (remaining + 1)

SOnlyResponseLabels.positiveStages_labelled (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (offset : Nat) :
  (∀ (index : Nat),
      index ≤ offset + 1 →
        (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program index (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠ []) →
    ∃ stages,
      SOnlyResponseLabels.SourceLabels program dispatcher (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix)) (offset + 1)
        stages.configurations
'SOnlyResponseLabels.positiveStages_labelled' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyResponseLabels.contractionRun_label_has_source (bits : List Bool) (index : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ index + 1 →
        (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])
  (label : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionLabel SOnly38.program)
  (observed :
    SOnlyResponseLabels.responseLabel? SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher
        ((SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem bits).contractionRun (index + 1)) =
      some label) :
  ∃ sourceIndex,
    sourceIndex < index + 1 ∧
      SOnlyResponseLabels.sourceLabel? SOnly38.program
          (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program sourceIndex (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)) =
        some label
'SOnlyResponseLabels.contractionRun_label_has_source' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyResponseLabels.no_target_script_on_event_free_run (bits : List Bool)
  (nonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat), (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])

SOnlyResponseLabels.stageStart_labelled (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ []) :
  ∃ parents configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerRecurrence.stageSourceConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits (r + 1)
          parents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits)
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length = SOnlyStageEvents.stageStartTime bits r ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher parents
            (PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointRun.cumulativeLayers r) ∧
          SOnlyResponseLabels.SourceLabels SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher
            (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) r configurations
'SOnlyResponseLabels.stageStart_labelled' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyResponseLabels.firstJob_response_prefix_labelled (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r →
        (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠
          []) :
  have bits := bit :: suffix;
  have environment :=
    PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree) bits;
  let continuation := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit (r + 1) r environment;
  ∃ parents registers phase responseBit responseSuffix source outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext ticks
    configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher registers
          PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
            (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits
                  continuation) ::
              parents)))
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits)
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length = SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent registers phase [] false ∧
          PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits continuation
              source ⋯ registers responseBit responseSuffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents
              ticks ∧
            { phase := phase, data := responseBit :: responseSuffix } =
                PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) ∧
              SOnlyResponseLabels.SourceLabels SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher
                (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) r configurations
'SOnlyResponseLabels.firstJob_response_prefix_labelled' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyResponseLabels.no_target_script_before_firstJob_response (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])
  (noEvent :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k < r →
        ¬SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)))

    SOnlyResponseLabels.responseLabel? SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher

      some SOnly38.targetLabel

  {registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program} {bit : Bool} {seedBits : List Bool}
  {continuation carrier : PureSFormal.PureS.Term} {parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame}
  {configurations : List (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher)}
  {entries : List (Bool × PureSFormal.PureS.Term)}
  (pairs :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.ResponseSamplePairs program dispatcher registers bit seedBits continuation carrier
      parents configurations entries)
  (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)

  (hv : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed carrier)
  (outer : SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Wraps (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) parents)
  (origin : allowed (registers.phase, bit) carrier) :
  SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed)
    configurations

SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedResponse_licensedChain (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  (seedBits : List Bool) (continuation source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (ticks : Nat)
  (trace :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source admissible
      registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents ticks)
  (notSeen : registers.seen = false)
  (hb :
    SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Ordinary (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) continuation)
  (hs : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed source)
  (outer : SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Wraps (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) parents)
  (origin :
    allowed ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit)
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext)) :
  ∃ configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.selectedReturnConfiguration program dispatcher registers bit suffix seedBits
          continuation (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext) parents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
            (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
              parents)))
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length =
          1 +
            PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completedCost program
              (dispatcher.route ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit))
              ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit) ∧
        SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed)
          configurations
'SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedResponse_licensedChain' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedResponse_licensed (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  (seedBits : List Bool) (continuation source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (ticks : Nat)
  (trace :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source admissible
      registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents ticks)
  (notSeen : registers.seen = false)
  (hb :
    SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Ordinary (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) continuation)
  (hs : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed source)
  (outer : SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Wraps (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) parents)
  (origin :
    allowed ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit)
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier bit outerContext innerContext))
  {terminal : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher}
  {configurations : List (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher)}
  (chain :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher) terminal
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers PureSFormal.PureS.omega
        (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
              (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits continuation) ::
            parents)))
      configurations)
  (count :
    configurations.length =
      1 +
        PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completedCost program
          (dispatcher.route ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit))
          ((PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers bit suffix).phase, bit)) :
  SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed)
    configurations

SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedNonemptyPendingPrefix_licensed (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (inputBits seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (hadmissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (outerParents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (layers : Nat)
  (outer : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher outerParents layers)
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  (hb :
    SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Ordinary (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) continuation)
  (outerLicensed :
    SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Wraps (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) outerParents)
  (remaining sampleIndex : Nat) (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program)
  (phase : PureSFormal.CTS.Phase program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context) (ticks : Nat) :
  PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent registers phase [] false →
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation source hadmissible
        registers bit suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext
        (PureSFormal.PureS.PrimitiveFuel.pendingParents
          (PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree) seedBits)
          continuation remaining outerParents)
        ticks →
      (∀ (k : Nat),
          k ≤ remaining → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program k { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix }).data ≠ []) →
        SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed source →
          SOnlyGlobalOrigins.SourceAllowed allowed { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } remaining →
            ∃ finalRegisters finalPhase finalBit finalSuffix finalSource finalOuterContext finalFullContext
              finalInnerContext finalTargetContext finalTicks configurations,
              PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher finalRegisters
                    PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                    (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames finalFullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                      (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits
                            continuation) ::
                        outerParents)))
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                    PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                    (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                      (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits
                            continuation) ::
                        PureSFormal.PureS.PrimitiveFuel.pendingParents
                          (PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree)
                            seedBits)
                          continuation remaining outerParents)))
                  configurations ∧
                PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.IndexedResponseSampledStates program dispatcher inputBits sampleIndex
                    configurations ∧
                  configurations.length =
                      PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedResponse.nonemptySweepCost program dispatcher remaining
                        { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } ∧
                    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent finalRegisters finalPhase [] false ∧
                      PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits continuation
                          finalSource hadmissible finalRegisters finalBit finalSuffix finalOuterContext finalFullContext
                          finalInnerContext finalTargetContext outerParents finalTicks ∧
                        { phase := finalPhase, data := finalBit :: finalSuffix } =
                            PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program remaining { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } ∧
                          SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed
                              (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) configurations ∧
                            SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed finalSource
'SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedNonemptyPendingPrefix_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedNonemptySweepToFreshCheck_licensed (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (inputBits seedBits : List Bool) (stage jobs : Nat)
  (environment : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (outerParents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (layers : Nat)
  (outer : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher outerParents layers)
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  (he : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed environment)
  (outerLicensed : SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed outerParents)
  (remaining sampleIndex : Nat) (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program)
  (phase : PureSFormal.CTS.Phase program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context) (ticks : Nat) :
  PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent registers phase [] false →
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace program dispatcher seedBits
        (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment) source ⋯ registers bit suffix outerContext
        fullContext innerContext targetContext
        (PureSFormal.PureS.PrimitiveFuel.pendingParents
          (PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree) seedBits)
          (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment) remaining outerParents)
        ticks →
      (∀ (k : Nat),
          k ≤ remaining + 1 → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program k { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix }).data ≠ []) →
        SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed source →
          SOnlyGlobalOrigins.SourceAllowed allowed { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } (remaining + 1) →
            ∃ nextParents checkRegisters configurations,
              PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerContinuation.checkConfiguration program dispatcher checkRegisters
                    { focus := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment,
                      parents := nextParents })
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration program dispatcher registers
                    PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                    (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
                      (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction program dispatcher seedBits
                            (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment)) ::
                        PureSFormal.PureS.PrimitiveFuel.pendingParents
                          (PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree)
                            seedBits)
                          (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment) remaining outerParents)))
                  configurations ∧
                PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.IndexedResponseSampledStates program dispatcher inputBits sampleIndex
                    configurations ∧
                  configurations.length =
                      PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedResponse.nonemptySweepCost program dispatcher (remaining + 1)
                        { phase := phase, data := bit :: suffix } ∧
                    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher nextParents
                        (layers + 1) ∧
                      SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed
                          (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) configurations ∧
                        SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed nextParents
'SOnlyGlobalOrigins.selectedNonemptySweepToFreshCheck_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalOrigins.completeNonemptyJob_licensed (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool)
  (stage jobs fuel sampleIndex : Nat) (environment : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (outerParents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (layers : Nat)
  (outer : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher outerParents layers)
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  (he : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed environment)
  (outerLicensed : SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed outerParents)
  (fuelPositive : fuel ≠ 0)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ fuel → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠ [])
  (sourceAllowed : SOnlyGlobalOrigins.SourceAllowed allowed (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix)) fuel) :
  ∃ nextParents checkRegisters configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerContinuation.checkConfiguration program dispatcher checkRegisters
          { focus := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment, parents := nextParents })
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedPhase.fuelTerminalConfigurationAt program dispatcher (bit :: suffix)
          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers.newJob program)
          (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment) outerParents fuel 0)
        configurations ∧
      PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.IndexedResponseSampledStates program dispatcher (bit :: suffix) sampleIndex
          configurations ∧
        configurations.length = PureSFormal.PureS.ExactCheckpointRun.jobCost program dispatcher (bit :: suffix) fuel ∧
          PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher nextParents (layers + 1) ∧
            (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerContinuation.checkConfiguration program dispatcher checkRegisters
                      { focus := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment,
                        parents := nextParents }).cursor.erase =
                PureSFormal.PureS.Cursor.rebuild nextParents
                  (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment) ∧
              (PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit stage (jobs + 1) environment).headArity = 3 ∧
                SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed
                    (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed) configurations ∧
                  SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed nextParents
'SOnlyGlobalOrigins.completeNonemptyJob_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalTerminal.completeNonemptyTerminalJobRaw_licensed (program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program)
  (dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program) (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (fuel sampleIndex : Nat)
  (outerParents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (layers : Nat)
  (outer : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents program dispatcher outerParents layers)
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  (outerLicensed : SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed outerParents)
  (fuelPositive : fuel ≠ 0)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ fuel → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠ [])
  (sourceAllowed : SOnlyGlobalOrigins.SourceAllowed allowed (PureSFormal.CTS.initial program (bit :: suffix)) fuel) :
  have bits := bit :: suffix;
  have environment := PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions program dispatcher.tree) bits;
  have continuation := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit fuel 0 environment;
  ∃ nextParents leading checkpoint configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine program dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerRootContinuation.nextStageSourceConfiguration program dispatcher fuel environment
          nextParents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedPhase.fuelTerminalConfigurationAt program dispatcher bits
          (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers.newJob program) continuation outerParents fuel 0)
        configurations ∧
      configurations = leading ++ [checkpoint] ∧
        configurations.length = PureSFormal.PureS.ExactCheckpointRun.jobCost program dispatcher bits fuel ∧
          PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerNestedResponse.FreshTerminalData program dispatcher bits fuel checkpoint
              nextParents (layers + 1) ∧
            (∀ {activeContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context}
                {checkpointChain : PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointDecoder.ChainView program},
                PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointRun.PositivePrefix program dispatcher bits fuel checkpoint.cursor.erase
                    (PureSFormal.PureS.ExactCheckpointRun.checkpointTime program dispatcher bits fuel) activeContext
                    checkpointChain →
                  sampleIndex + configurations.length =
                      PureSFormal.PureS.ExactCheckpointRun.checkpointTime program dispatcher bits fuel →
                    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.IndexedResponseSampledStates program dispatcher bits sampleIndex
                      configurations) ∧
              SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed)
                  configurations ∧
                SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed nextParents
'SOnlyGlobalTerminal.completeNonemptyTerminalJobRaw_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

SOnlyFirstEventResponse.responsePairs_licensed_split {program : PureSFormal.CTS.Program}
  {dispatcher : PureSFormal.PureS.ActionDispatcher program} (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet program)
  {registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers program} {bit : Bool} {bits : List Bool}
  {continuation carrier : PureSFormal.PureS.Term} {parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame}
  (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (held : PureSFormal.PureS.ReachableAudit.Holds program dispatcher.tree bits continuation carrier)
  (hb : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed continuation)
  (bc : SOnlyProvenance.classify continuation = SOnlyProvenance.State.other)
  (hv : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree allowed carrier)
  (outer : SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents program dispatcher allowed parents)
  {configurations : List (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration program dispatcher)}
  {entries : List (Bool × PureSFormal.PureS.Term)}
  (pairs :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.ResponseSamplePairs program dispatcher registers bit bits continuation carrier
      parents configurations entries)
  (flags : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.FinalResponseFlags entries) :
  ∃ leading checkpoint,
    configurations = leading ++ [checkpoint] ∧
      SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed program dispatcher.tree allowed)
          leading ∧
        SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed program dispatcher.tree
            (SOnlyFirstEventOrigins.AddOrigin allowed (registers.phase, bit) carrier) checkpoint.cursor.erase ∧
          checkpoint.cursor.erase =
            PureSFormal.PureS.Cursor.rebuild parents
              (PureSFormal.PureS.LocalResponse.completed bits continuation carrier
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.completedRoute program dispatcher registers bit carrier))

SOnlyGlobalFirstPrefix.stageStart_licensed (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet SOnly38.program)
  (sourceAllowed : SOnlyGlobalOrigins.SourceAllowed allowed (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) r) :
  ∃ parents configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerRecurrence.stageSourceConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits (r + 1)
          parents)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits)
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length = SOnlyStageEvents.stageStartTime bits r ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCompletedContext.CompletedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher parents
            (PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointRun.cumulativeLayers r) ∧
          SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed
              (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed) configurations ∧
            SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher allowed parents
'SOnlyGlobalFirstPrefix.stageStart_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalFirstPrefix.firstJob_response_prefix_licensed (bit : Bool) (suffix : List Bool) (r : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r →
        (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (bit :: suffix))).data ≠ [])
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet SOnly38.program)
  (sourceAllowed :
    SOnlyGlobalOrigins.SourceAllowed allowed (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (bit :: suffix)) r) :
  have bits := bit :: suffix;
  have environment :=
    PureSFormal.PureS.environmentCode (PureSFormal.PureS.compileActions SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree) bits;
  let continuation := PureSFormal.PureS.Dovetail.clockExit (r + 1) r environment;
  ∃ parents registers phase responseBit responseSuffix source outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext ticks
    configurations,
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponseInvariant.ExactMutationChain
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.upConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher registers
          PureSFormal.PureS.omega
          (PureSFormal.PureS.ContextCursor.frames fullContext PureSFormal.PureS.omega
            (PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame.right
                (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.pendingFunction SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits
                  continuation) ::
              parents)))
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.initialConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits)
        configurations ∧
      configurations.length = SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r ∧
        PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.RegistersCoherent registers phase [] false ∧
          PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher bits continuation
              source ⋯ registers responseBit responseSuffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents
              ticks ∧
            { phase := phase, data := responseBit :: responseSuffix } =
                PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) ∧
              SOnlySchedulerOrigins.SamplesLicensed
                  (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed) configurations ∧
                SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher allowed parents ∧
                  SOnlySchedulerOrigins.Ordinary
                      (SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.RootLicensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed)
                      continuation ∧
                    SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed source
'SOnlyGlobalFirstPrefix.firstJob_response_prefix_licensed' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalFirstEvent.licensed_prefix_silent (bits : List Bool)

  (chain :

      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher) terminal

SOnlyGlobalFirstEvent.sourceTarget_globally_first (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat) (eventSuffix : List Bool) (value : Nat)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])
  (noEvent :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k < r →
        ¬SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)))
  (event :
    PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) =
      { phase := SOnly38.targetPhase, data := true :: eventSuffix })
  (numerical : SOnlyResultBits.readMachineBits (true :: eventSuffix) = some value) :
  SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory bits (SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57)) = true ∧
    (∀ (index : Nat),
        index < SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57 →
          SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory bits index) = false) ∧
      (∀ (address : PureSFormal.PureS.Address) (shell : PureSFormal.PureS.Term),
          (SOnly38.trajectory bits (SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57)).subterm? address =
              some shell →
            SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true →
              SOnlyWitnessResult.readShell shell = some value) ∧
        SOnlyCurrentDecoder.read (SOnly38.trajectory bits (SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57)) =
          some value

SOnlyGlobalFirstStructure.target_response_structure (inputBits : List Bool)
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet SOnly38.program)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers SOnly38.program)
  (phaseEq : registers.phase = SOnly38.targetPhase) (seedBits : List Bool)
  (continuation carrier : PureSFormal.PureS.Term) (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame)
  (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (held : PureSFormal.PureS.ReachableAudit.Holds SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree seedBits continuation carrier)
  (hb : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed continuation)
  (bc : SOnlyProvenance.classify continuation = SOnlyProvenance.State.other)
  (hv : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed carrier)
  (outer : SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher allowed parents)
  (excludes : ∀ (audit : PureSFormal.PureS.Term), ¬allowed SOnly38.targetLabel audit) (suffix : List Bool)
  (decoded :
    PureSFormal.PureS.CarrierDecoder.decode? SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree seedBits continuation admissible
        carrier =
      some suffix)
  (sampleIndex bridgeTicks : Nat)
  (before : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
  (beforeEq : (SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem inputBits).contractionRun sampleIndex = before)
  (bridge :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.ZeroMutationRun
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher) bridgeTicks before
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.responseStartConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher registers true
        seedBits continuation carrier parents)) :
  (∀ (offset : Nat), offset < 56 → SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory inputBits (sampleIndex + offset)) = false) ∧
    SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory inputBits (sampleIndex + 56)) = true ∧
      ∀ (address : PureSFormal.PureS.Address) (shell : PureSFormal.PureS.Term),
        (SOnly38.trajectory inputBits (sampleIndex + 56)).subterm? address = some shell →
          SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true →
            shell.subterm? SOnlyEventTransfer.auditAddress = some carrier ∧
              SOnlyEventTransfer.readPrestate SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree true shell = some (true :: suffix)
'SOnlyGlobalFirstStructure.target_response_structure' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlyGlobalFirstStructure.selectedTarget_response_structure (inputBits seedBits : List Bool)
  (allowed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.OriginSet SOnly38.program) (continuation source : PureSFormal.PureS.Term)
  (admissible : PureSFormal.PureS.Carrier.Admissible continuation)
  (registers : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.Registers SOnly38.program)
  (phaseEq : registers.phase = SOnly38.targetPhase) (suffix : List Bool)
  (outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext : PureSFormal.PureS.Context)
  (parents : List PureSFormal.PureS.ParentFrame) (responseTicks : Nat)
  (response :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.SelectedResponseTrace SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher seedBits continuation
      source admissible registers true suffix outerContext fullContext innerContext targetContext parents responseTicks)
  (hb : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed continuation)
  (bc : SOnlyProvenance.classify continuation = SOnlyProvenance.State.other)
  (sourceLicensed : SOnlyGeneratedOrigins.Licensed SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree allowed source)
  (outer : SOnlyGlobalAncestors.LicensedParents SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher allowed parents)
  (excludes : ∀ (audit : PureSFormal.PureS.Term), ¬allowed SOnly38.targetLabel audit) (sampleIndex bridgeTicks : Nat)
  (before : PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.Configuration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher)
  (beforeEq : (SOnlyEventTransfer.persistentSystem inputBits).contractionRun sampleIndex = before)
  (bridge :
    PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerInvariant.ZeroMutationRun
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerControl.machine SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher) bridgeTicks before
      (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerResponse.responseStartConfiguration SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.scannedRegisters registers true suffix) true seedBits continuation
        (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier true outerContext innerContext) parents)) :
  (∀ (offset : Nat), offset < 56 → SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory inputBits (sampleIndex + offset)) = false) ∧
    SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory inputBits (sampleIndex + 56)) = true ∧
      ∀ (address : PureSFormal.PureS.Address) (shell : PureSFormal.PureS.Term),
        (SOnly38.trajectory inputBits (sampleIndex + 56)).subterm? address = some shell →
          SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true →
            shell.subterm? SOnlyEventTransfer.auditAddress =
                some (PureSFormal.PureS.SchedulerCycle.deletedCarrier true outerContext innerContext) ∧
              SOnlyEventTransfer.readPrestate SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree true shell = some (true :: suffix)

SOnlyGlobalFirstStructure.sourceTarget_first_structure (bits : List Bool) (r : Nat) (eventSuffix : List Bool)
  (allNonempty :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k ≤ r → (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data ≠ [])
  (noEvent :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k < r →
        ¬SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)))
  (event :
    PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits) =
      { phase := SOnly38.targetPhase, data := true :: eventSuffix }) :
  SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory bits (SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57)) = true ∧
    (∀ (index : Nat),
        index < SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57 →
          SOnlyObserver.event (SOnly38.trajectory bits index) = false) ∧
      ∃ snapshot,
        PureSFormal.PureS.CheckpointDecoder.decodeCarrier? SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree snapshot =
            some eventSuffix ∧
          ∀ (address : PureSFormal.PureS.Address) (shell : PureSFormal.PureS.Term),
            (SOnly38.trajectory bits (SOnlyStageEvents.firstJobPreResponseTime bits r + 57)).subterm? address =
                some shell →
              SOnlyEventPattern.targetPattern.matchesBool shell = true →
                shell.subterm? SOnlyEventTransfer.auditAddress = some snapshot ∧
                  SOnlyEventTransfer.readPrestate SOnly38.program SOnly38.dispatcher.tree true shell =
                    some (true :: eventSuffix)

  (noEvent :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      k < r →
        ¬SOnlySource.Event (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program k (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)))

                    some (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program r (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program bits)).data

SOnlyMachineClock.clockList {α : Type} : List α → List (SOnlyMachineClock.Clock α)
'SOnlyMachineClock.clockList' does not depend on any axioms
SOnlyMachineClock.mem_clockList {α : Type} [DecidableEq α] (items : List α) (c : SOnlyMachineClock.Clock α) :
  c ∈ SOnlyMachineClock.clockList items ↔
    match c with
    | SOnlyMachineClock.Clock.data i => i ∈ items
    | SOnlyMachineClock.Clock.recovery i => i ∈ items
    | SOnlyMachineClock.Clock.action (SOnlyMachine.Action.inc i) => i ∈ items
    | SOnlyMachineClock.Clock.action (SOnlyMachine.Action.dec i) => i ∈ items
    | SOnlyMachineClock.Clock.action SOnlyMachine.Action.halt => True
'SOnlyMachineClock.mem_clockList' depends on axioms: [propext]

SOnlyMachineClock.halt_output {α : Type} [DecidableEq α] (P : SOnlyMachine.Table α) (e : SOnlyMachineBP2.Enumeration α)

  (hk :
    SOnlyMachineBP2.Halted (SOnlyMachineClock.program P e initial)
      (SOnlyMachineBP2.run (SOnlyMachineClock.program P e initial) k { pc := 0, value := fun x => 0 }))
  (output : α) :
  (SOnlyMachine.run P k initial).action = SOnlyMachine.Action.halt ∧
    (SOnlyMachineBP2.run (SOnlyMachineClock.program P e initial) k { pc := 0, value := fun x => 0 }).value
        (SOnlyMachineWaterfallBP2.Cell.value (SOnlyMachineClock.Clock.data output)) =
      2 * (SOnlyMachine.run P k initial).value output + 3

SOnlyMachineCorrectness.output_iff {d n : Nat} (M : SOnlyMachine.Machine d n) (input : Fin (d + 1) → Nat) (v : Nat) :
  (∃ time,
      SOnlyMachineBP2.Halted (SOnlyMachineNat.compile M input)
          (SOnlyMachineBP2.run (SOnlyMachineNat.compile M input) time SOnlyMachineNat.initialState) ∧
        (SOnlyMachineBP2.run (SOnlyMachineNat.compile M input) time SOnlyMachineNat.initialState).value 0 = 2 * v + 3) ↔
    ∃ steps,
      M.instruction (SOnlyMachine.sourceRun M steps (SOnlyMachine.initial M input)).label =
          SOnlyMachine.Instruction.halt ∧
        (SOnlyMachine.sourceRun M steps (SOnlyMachine.initial M input)).value 0 = v

  SOnlyTemporalMemory.observe (SOnlyTemporalMemory.run shift (SOnlyTemporalMemory.forcedRun forcing time xs)) =
    (SOnlyTemporalMemory.observe (SOnlyTemporalMemory.run (shift + time) xs) ^^
      SOnlyTemporalMemory.response depth forcing time shift)
'SOnlyTemporalMemory.forcing_response' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (hlen : xs.length = 2 ^ depth) (htime : time < 2 ^ depth) :
  SOnlyTemporalMemory.observe (SOnlyTemporalMemory.forcedRun forcing time xs) =
    SOnlyTemporalMemory.observe (SOnlyTemporalMemory.run time xs)

SOnlyTemporalMemory.first_disturbed_output (depth : Nat) :
  (∀ (time : Nat),
      time < 2 ^ depth →
        SOnlyTemporalMemory.observe

          false) ∧

      true

  (hbit : bit < depth) :
  SOnlyTemporalMemory.lookup (SOnlyTemporalMemory.stage bit v) index =
    if index.testBit bit = true then SOnlyTemporalMemory.lookup v index
    else SOnlyTemporalMemory.lookup v index ^^ SOnlyTemporalMemory.lookup v (index + 2 ^ bit)

          SOnlySimulation.incrementPrefix (List.map (fun row => (SOnlyMemory.reset row.fst, row.snd + 1)) after)

  (target last : List SOnlyMemory.Cell) (q p : Bool) (htarget : target ≠ [])
  (hbefore : ∀ (row : SOnlySimulation.IncrementRow), row ∈ before → row.fst ≠ []) :
  SOnlySimulation.exceptionalWord before after target last ≠ [] ∧
    SOnlyCounter.pass q (SOnlySimulation.exceptionalWord before after target last) ≠ [] ∧
      SOnlyCounter.pass p (SOnlyCounter.pass q (SOnlySimulation.exceptionalWord before after target last)) ≠ []
'SOnlySimulation.exceptional_nonempty' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

  SOnlySimulation.SafeRun { word := SOnlySimulation.exceptionalWord before after target last, take := q }

      take := false }

  (next : ∀ (b : Nat), b < n → entry (b + 1) = (action b).take)
  (width : ∀ (b : Nat), b < n → SOnlyMemory.widthParity (mem b) = (entry b ^^ (action b).take))

SOnlySimulation.indexed_exceptional_word (mem : Nat → List SOnlyMemory.Cell) (counter : Nat → List SOnlySource.Symbol)
  (exponent : Nat → Nat) (count target : Nat) (ht : target < count + 1)
  (hc : ∀ (b : Nat), b < count + 1 → b ≠ target → counter b = SOnlyCounter.C (exponent b)) :
