      (SOnlyMemory.memory (mem target) ++

SOnlySimulation.indexedIncrement_fits (mem : Nat → List SOnlyMemory.Cell) (exponent : Nat → Nat) (entry : Nat → Bool)
  (n base : Nat) (next : ∀ (b : Nat), base ≤ b → b < base + n → entry (b + 1) = true)
  (width : ∀ (b : Nat), base ≤ b → b < base + n → SOnlyMemory.widthParity (mem b) = (entry b ^^ true)) :
  SOnlySimulation.IncrementFits (entry base) (SOnlySimulation.indexedIncrementRows mem exponent n base) ∧
    SOnlySimulation.incrementEnd (entry base) (SOnlySimulation.indexedIncrementRows mem exponent n base) =
      entry (base + n)
'SOnlySimulation.indexedIncrement_fits' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
SOnlySimulation.scheduled_exceptional_fits (depth count : Nat) (program : SOnlySimulation.BPProgram) (time target : Nat)
  (exponent : Nat → Nat) (ht : target < count + 1) (horizon : time < 2 ^ depth)
  (actions : ∀ (b : Nat), b < count + 1 → SOnlySimulation.scheduledAction count program time b = decide (b ≠ target)) :
  SOnlySimulation.ExceptionalFits (SOnlySimulation.scheduledEntry count program time 0)
    (SOnlySimulation.scheduledParity program time)
    (SOnlySimulation.indexedIncrementRows (SOnlySimulation.programMemory depth count program time) exponent target 0)
    (SOnlySimulation.indexedIncrementRows (SOnlySimulation.programMemory depth count program time) exponent
      (count - target) (target + 1))
    (SOnlySimulation.programMemory depth count program time target)
    (SOnlySimulation.programMemory depth count program time (count + 1))
'SOnlySimulation.scheduled_exceptional_fits' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
SOnlySimulation.scheduled_zero_reset_run (depth count : Nat) (program : SOnlySimulation.BPProgram) (time target : Nat)
  (value : Nat → Nat) (ht : target < count + 1) (zero : value target = 0) (horizon : time < 2 ^ depth)
  (actions : ∀ (b : Nat), b < count + 1 → SOnlySimulation.scheduledAction count program time b = decide (b ≠ target))
  (parity : SOnlySimulation.scheduledParity program time = true) :
  SOnlySimulation.SafeRun
    { word := SOnlySimulation.programWord depth count program time fun b => SOnlyCounter.C (2 * value b),
      take := SOnlySimulation.scheduledEntry count program time 0 }
    { word := SOnlySimulation.programWord depth count program 0 (SOnlySimulation.protectedWords value target),
      take := false }

SOnlySimulation.nonhalting_prefix_progress (depth count : Nat) (program : SOnlySimulation.BPProgram)
  (valid : SOnlySimulation.WellFormed count program) (capacity : 2 * List.length program + 3 < 2 ^ depth)
  (nonhalt : ∀ (n : Nat), ¬SOnlyMachineBP2.Halted program (SOnlyMachineBP2.run program n SOnlySimulation.initialState))
  (n : Nat) :
  ∃ steps,
    n < steps ∧
      SOnlySource.iterate steps { word := SOnlySimulation.seed depth count program, take := true } =
          SOnlySimulation.sourceConfig depth count program
            (SOnlyMachineBP2.run program n SOnlySimulation.initialState) ∧
        ∀ (time : Nat),
          time < steps →
            ¬SOnlySource.Selected
                  (SOnlySource.iterate time { word := SOnlySimulation.seed depth count program, take := true }) ∧
              (SOnlySource.iterate time { word := SOnlySimulation.seed depth count program, take := true }).word ≠ []

SOnlySimulation.machine_result_iff_first_cts_result {d n : Nat} (M : SOnlyMachine.Machine d n)
  (input : Fin (d + 1) → Nat) (value : Nat) :
  (∃ steps,
      M.instruction (SOnlyMachine.sourceRun M steps (SOnlyMachine.initial M input)).label =
          SOnlyMachine.Instruction.halt ∧
        (SOnlyMachine.sourceRun M steps (SOnlyMachine.initial M input)).value 0 = value) ↔
    ∃ time,
      SOnlySource.Event
          (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program time
            (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (SOnlySimulation.machineBits M input))) ∧
        (∀ (earlier : Nat),
            earlier < time →
              ¬SOnlySource.Event
                  (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program earlier
                    (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (SOnlySimulation.machineBits M input)))) ∧
          SOnlyResultBits.readMachineBits
              (PureSFormal.CTS.iterate SOnly38.program time
                  (PureSFormal.CTS.initial SOnly38.program (SOnlySimulation.machineBits M input))).data =

SOnlySimulation.published_width_difference (count : Nat) (instruction : SOnlySimulation.Macro) (second : Bool)
  (block : Nat) (hb : block ≤ count + 1) (valid : SOnlySimulation.Macro.Bounded count instruction) :
  SOnlySimulation.publishedWidth count instruction second block =
    ((if block = 0 then SOnlySimulation.publishedEntry instruction second
      else SOnlySimulation.macroAction count instruction second (block - 1)) ^^
      if block < count + 1 then SOnlySimulation.macroAction count instruction second block
      else SOnlySimulation.publishedParity instruction second)

SOnlyTapeCompiler.input_left_stack_result_iff {states symbols : Nat} (m : SOnlyTapeStacks.Machine states symbols)
  (input : List (Fin symbols)) (result : Nat) :
  (∃ t,
      (SOnlyTapeCompiler.inputMachine m input).instruction
            (SOnlyMachine.sourceRun (SOnlyTapeCompiler.inputMachine m input) t
                (SOnlyMachine.initial (SOnlyTapeCompiler.inputMachine m input)
                  (SOnlyTapeCompiler.inputValues symbols input))).label =

                (SOnlyMachine.initial (SOnlyTapeCompiler.inputMachine m input)

          result) ↔

      SOnlyTapeStacks.run (SOnlyTapeStacks.stackStep m) n (SOnlyTapeStacks.stackInput m input) = some stacks ∧
        SOnlyTapeStacks.stackStep m stacks = none ∧ SOnlyTapeStacks.stackCode symbols stacks.left = result
